style="" hs-cos-general-type="widget" hs-cos-type="rich_text"></span>

InOrbit is a distributed, cloud-based RobOps (robot operations) platform
that enables teams deploying robots to get to market faster with a
robust product, and takes the stress out of scaling, at any stage. It's
easy to get started receiving real-time data from your robots. Almost
everything is customizable and extensible.

InOrbit offers several editions based on your needs. Click
[here](https://www.inorbit.ai/pricing?hsLang=en) for more Information. 

 <img src="https://www.inorbit.ai/hubfs/Group%201900.svg"
style="width: 48px; float: left;" loading="lazy" width="48"
alt="Group 1900" /><span style="color: #807575;">  
Free functional version of InOrbit with sufficient features to support
basic RobOps</span>  
<span style="color: #807575;"></span>

<span style="color: #807575;"><img src="https://www.inorbit.ai/hubfs/Group%201901.svg"
style="width: 77px; float: left;" loading="lazy" width="77"
alt="Group 1901" />  
Paid InOrbit version with a broader set of complete features, along with
some limited versions of more advanced features</span>

<span style="color: #807575; background-color: transparent;"><img src="https://www.inorbit.ai/hubfs/Group%201899.svg"
style="width: 86px; float: left;" loading="lazy" width="86"
alt="Group 1899" />  
Special paid version of InOrbit with all features included, designed for
companies that are looking to maximize RobOps capabilities</span>

<span style="color: #807575; background-color: transparent;"><img src="https://www.inorbit.ai/hubfs/label%20developer%20edition.svg"
style="height: auto; max-width: 100%; width: 86px;" loading="lazy"
width="86" height="17" alt="label developer edition" />  
Leverage the full scope of the platform for a small number of robots.
<a href="https://developer.inorbit.ai/pricing-dev?hsLang=en"
rel="noopener">Learn more</a>.</span>

Read on to learn how to get the most out of InOrbit. Need some extra
help? Contact us at <support@inorbit.ai>

</div>

<div class="hs-accordion">

<div id="button-container-id-look-at-inorbit" class="hs-accordion__item"
aria-expanded="">

<span id="look-at-inorbit" class="anchor"></span>

A look at InOrbit Control <span class="hs-accordion__item-icon"></span>

<div role="main">

<div class="hs-accordion__item-content">

[InOrbit
Control](https://control.inorbit.ai/)<span style="color: #000000;"> is a
tool for managing robots and viewing robot data. InOrbit Control is
based on dashboards that make it easy to personalize the user experience
with rich visualization and interactive robot controls.</span>

<span style="color: #000000;">You have full control over creating
</span>**<span style="color: #000000;">dashboards</span>**<span style="color: #000000;">
and setting visibility and permissions in order to personalize the
experience to different user roles.</span>

###### *<span style="color: #666666;">Control and Dashboard Overview</span>*

<span style="color: #000000;">Your InOrbit Control account can have
multiple
</span>**<span style="color: #000000;">dashboards</span>**<span style="color: #000000;">,
which provide targeted experiences for every
</span>**<span style="color: #000000;">role</span>**<span style="color: #000000;">
on your team.</span>

<span style="color: #000000;">The following **dashboards** are the
default set for your account, which can be modified:</span>

- <span style="color: #000000;">Locations</span>
- <span style="color: #000000;">Fleet</span>  
  <span style="color: #000000;"></span>
- <span style="color: #000000;">Robot</span>
- <span style="color: #000000;">Navigation</span>

<span style="color: #000000;">These are just a starting point. As an
Owner or Administrator, you can customize any dashboard.</span>

<span style="color: #000000;">With these roles, you can also
<a href="https://www.inorbit.ai/docs?hsLang=en#create-dashboards"
style="color: #000000;">create your own dashboards</a>\* from
scratch.</span>

<span style="color: #000000;">The following default roles have been
created for your account.</span>

- <span style="color: #000000;">Owner</span>
- <span style="color: #000000;">Administrator\*</span>
- <span style="color: #000000;">Manager</span>
- <span style="color: #000000;">Engineer\*</span>
- <span style="color: #000000;">Operator\*</span>
- <span style="color: #000000;">Viewer\*</span>

<span style="color: #000000;">\*Available in Standard and Enterprise
Editions</span>

###### *<span style="color: #666666;">Who sees which dashboards?</span>*

<span style="color: #000000;">Depending on a user’s role, they will have
access to different dashboards. For example,
</span><span style="color: #000000;">someone with the Operator role will
see:</span>

<span style="color: #000000;"><img
src="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/Screenshot%20from%202025-05-19%2010-12-00.png?width=290&amp;height=63&amp;name=Screenshot%20from%202025-05-19%2010-12-00.png"
style="width: 290px; height: auto; max-width: 100%;" loading="lazy"
srcset="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/Screenshot%20from%202025-05-19%2010-12-00.png?width=145&amp;height=32&amp;name=Screenshot%20from%202025-05-19%2010-12-00.png 145w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/Screenshot%20from%202025-05-19%2010-12-00.png?width=290&amp;height=63&amp;name=Screenshot%20from%202025-05-19%2010-12-00.png 290w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/Screenshot%20from%202025-05-19%2010-12-00.png?width=435&amp;height=95&amp;name=Screenshot%20from%202025-05-19%2010-12-00.png 435w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/Screenshot%20from%202025-05-19%2010-12-00.png?width=580&amp;height=126&amp;name=Screenshot%20from%202025-05-19%2010-12-00.png 580w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/Screenshot%20from%202025-05-19%2010-12-00.png?width=725&amp;height=158&amp;name=Screenshot%20from%202025-05-19%2010-12-00.png 725w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/Screenshot%20from%202025-05-19%2010-12-00.png?width=870&amp;height=189&amp;name=Screenshot%20from%202025-05-19%2010-12-00.png 870w"
sizes="(max-width: 290px) 100vw, 290px" width="290" height="63"
alt="Example list of dashboards. Includes Locations, Fleet, Robot, and Navigation dashboards." /></span>

###### *<span style="color: #666666;">Locations dashboard</span>*

The locations dashboard allows you to configure powerful operational
rules for your sites. Each location is made up of areas which represent
different parts of your facility like different
floors.<span style="color: #666666;"></span>

<img
src="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/locations-dashboard.png?width=600&amp;height=310&amp;name=locations-dashboard.png"
style="height: auto; max-width: 100%; width: 600px;" loading="lazy"
srcset="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/locations-dashboard.png?width=300&amp;height=155&amp;name=locations-dashboard.png 300w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/locations-dashboard.png?width=600&amp;height=310&amp;name=locations-dashboard.png 600w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/locations-dashboard.png?width=900&amp;height=465&amp;name=locations-dashboard.png 900w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/locations-dashboard.png?width=1200&amp;height=620&amp;name=locations-dashboard.png 1200w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/locations-dashboard.png?width=1500&amp;height=775&amp;name=locations-dashboard.png 1500w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/locations-dashboard.png?width=1800&amp;height=930&amp;name=locations-dashboard.png 1800w"
sizes="(max-width: 600px) 100vw, 600px" width="600" height="310"
alt="locations-dashboard" />

Selecting a location will take you to the locations editor widget. Here
you can add new areas to your location. For each area, you can add
maps from an image or directly from one of your robots. You can select a
default area for the location, and a default map for each area. 

<img
src="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/locations-editor.png?width=600&amp;height=310&amp;name=locations-editor.png"
style="height: auto; max-width: 100%; width: 600px;" loading="lazy"
srcset="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/locations-editor.png?width=300&amp;height=155&amp;name=locations-editor.png 300w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/locations-editor.png?width=600&amp;height=310&amp;name=locations-editor.png 600w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/locations-editor.png?width=900&amp;height=465&amp;name=locations-editor.png 900w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/locations-editor.png?width=1200&amp;height=620&amp;name=locations-editor.png 1200w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/locations-editor.png?width=1500&amp;height=775&amp;name=locations-editor.png 1500w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/locations-editor.png?width=1800&amp;height=930&amp;name=locations-editor.png 1800w"
sizes="(max-width: 600px) 100vw, 600px" width="600" height="310"
alt="locations-editor" />

Once you have a default map, you can annotate the map waypoints and
zones.  

To view multiple robots on a shared map, you can use the Map Aligner
tool to visually align a robot map to the default map. 

###### *<span style="color: #666666;">Fleet dashboard</span>*

<span style="color: #000000;">The</span>**<span style="color: #000000;">
Fleet dashboard</span>**<span style="color: #000000;"> is one of
InOrbit’s default
</span>**<span style="color: #000000;">dashboards</span>**<span style="color: #000000;">.
It shows an overview of your real-time fleet health.
</span><span style="color: #000000;"></span>

**<img
src="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20A%20look%20at%20InOrbit%20Control/Fleet/fleet_overview_wide.png?width=600&amp;name=fleet_overview_wide.png"
style="width: 600px;" loading="lazy"
srcset="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20A%20look%20at%20InOrbit%20Control/Fleet/fleet_overview_wide.png?width=300&amp;name=fleet_overview_wide.png 300w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20A%20look%20at%20InOrbit%20Control/Fleet/fleet_overview_wide.png?width=600&amp;name=fleet_overview_wide.png 600w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20A%20look%20at%20InOrbit%20Control/Fleet/fleet_overview_wide.png?width=900&amp;name=fleet_overview_wide.png 900w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20A%20look%20at%20InOrbit%20Control/Fleet/fleet_overview_wide.png?width=1200&amp;name=fleet_overview_wide.png 1200w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20A%20look%20at%20InOrbit%20Control/Fleet/fleet_overview_wide.png?width=1500&amp;name=fleet_overview_wide.png 1500w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20A%20look%20at%20InOrbit%20Control/Fleet/fleet_overview_wide.png?width=1800&amp;name=fleet_overview_wide.png 1800w"
sizes="(max-width: 600px) 100vw, 600px" width="600"
alt="fleet_overview_wide" />**

<span style="color: #000000;">The Robot Fleet View widget is a table
that displays a general overview of your robots, based on certain
characteristics, described below. In the default sort (by status),
robots with **errors** and **warnings** (see [Incident management -
incident
definitions](https://www.inorbit.ai/docs?hsLang=en#incident-management))
are always on the far left to give immediate visibility to robots that
may need attention. You can change the view of your fleet to group,
filter, and sort by certain criteria so you can focus on specific
**Collections** of robots (see [Collections, Locations,
Modes](https://www.inorbit.ai/docs?hsLang=en#collections-locations-modes)).</span>

<span style="color: #000000;"><img
src="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20A%20look%20at%20InOrbit%20Control/Fleet/fleet_wide.png?width=600&amp;name=fleet_wide.png"
style="width: 600px;" loading="lazy"
srcset="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20A%20look%20at%20InOrbit%20Control/Fleet/fleet_wide.png?width=300&amp;name=fleet_wide.png 300w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20A%20look%20at%20InOrbit%20Control/Fleet/fleet_wide.png?width=600&amp;name=fleet_wide.png 600w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20A%20look%20at%20InOrbit%20Control/Fleet/fleet_wide.png?width=900&amp;name=fleet_wide.png 900w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20A%20look%20at%20InOrbit%20Control/Fleet/fleet_wide.png?width=1200&amp;name=fleet_wide.png 1200w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20A%20look%20at%20InOrbit%20Control/Fleet/fleet_wide.png?width=1500&amp;name=fleet_wide.png 1500w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20A%20look%20at%20InOrbit%20Control/Fleet/fleet_wide.png?width=1800&amp;name=fleet_wide.png 1800w"
sizes="(max-width: 600px) 100vw, 600px" width="600" alt="fleet_wide" /></span>

|  |  |
|----|----|
| **<span style="color: #ffffff;">Section</span>** | **<span style="color: #ffffff;">Description</span>** |
| <span style="color: #807575;">Fleet</span> | <span style="color: #807575;">If robot collections have been defined, use the collections pulldown menu to select the collection you want to see. (A collection of robots is a group that you define for easier management. By default there are no collections, so Fleet View shows all robots.) You can group the fleet view by the following fields: Ungrouped (default), Location, Hardware, Customer, and Mode. Without customizing the fields, the groupings have no effect. Mode has some defaults: idle, charging, on mission, and more.</span> |
| <span style="color: #807575;">Status</span> | <span style="color: #807575;">On the left of the table are the characteristics of the robots for each row of the table. The default characteristics are Battery, Disk Usage, RAM usage, Diagnostics, Localization, ROS Status, and payload weight.</span> |
| <span style="color: #807575;">Robot list</span> | <span style="color: #807575;">The columns of the table list the color-coded status of the characteristics of individual robots. Red is error, yellow is warning, and blue is OK.</span> |
| <span style="color: #807575;">Robot names</span> | <span style="color: #807575;">Names of robots are below the table. Use the scroll bar at the bottom to see the status of other robots in the fleet. Beneath each robot name, the mode of the robot is displayed.</span> |

#####  

###### *<span style="color: #666666;">Robot dashboard</span>*

The **Robot dashboard** is a view of the most specific details about a
selected robot using visualization widgets, such as **Vitals**,
**Modes** **and** **Tags**, **Map**, etc. You can also perform certain
actions on a robot (see [Configure
actions](https://www.inorbit.ai/docs?hsLang=en#configure-actions) for
more information).

**<span style="color: #000000;"><img
src="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20A%20look%20at%20InOrbit%20Control/Robot/robot_overview_stretch.png.png?width=600&amp;name=robot_overview_stretch.png.png"
style="width: 600px;"
srcset="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20A%20look%20at%20InOrbit%20Control/Robot/robot_overview_stretch.png.png?width=300&amp;name=robot_overview_stretch.png.png 300w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20A%20look%20at%20InOrbit%20Control/Robot/robot_overview_stretch.png.png?width=600&amp;name=robot_overview_stretch.png.png 600w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20A%20look%20at%20InOrbit%20Control/Robot/robot_overview_stretch.png.png?width=900&amp;name=robot_overview_stretch.png.png 900w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20A%20look%20at%20InOrbit%20Control/Robot/robot_overview_stretch.png.png?width=1200&amp;name=robot_overview_stretch.png.png 1200w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20A%20look%20at%20InOrbit%20Control/Robot/robot_overview_stretch.png.png?width=1500&amp;name=robot_overview_stretch.png.png 1500w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20A%20look%20at%20InOrbit%20Control/Robot/robot_overview_stretch.png.png?width=1800&amp;name=robot_overview_stretch.png.png 1800w"
sizes="(max-width: 600px) 100vw, 600px" width="600"
alt="robot_overview_stretch.png" /></span>**

In the ROBOT / field, enter the name of the robot whose details you want
to see. Across that row are actions that can be taken or shortcuts to
other elements of the Control user interface, e.g., RESTART AGENT,
UPDATE AGENT, NAVIGATION, UPDATE AGENT, SETTINGS).

Some of the default widgets in individual robot details include the
following:

- Vitals
- Modes and Tags
- Timelines (Operating Metrics, Network)
- Actions
- Robot Log

The list and order of the widgets can be customized to your liking.

###### *Show “Live” data in your widgets *

InOrbit Control shows all the robot information in real-time by default,
indicated by blue "Live" indicators. But some widgets allow you to
select a time range in the past. When you do, it switches the data from
real-time to "Live off", indicated by a gray indicator. In this case,
clicking on the "Live off" indicator takes you back to a default "Live"
mode where all the data is real-time, and removes the time range filter
you applied.

This is the timeline widget with the “Live” switch on:

<span style="font-size: 11px;"><img
src="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20A%20look%20at%20InOrbit%20Control/Robot/live_button_on_timeline.png?width=600&amp;name=live_button_on_timeline.png"
style="width: 600px;" loading="lazy"
srcset="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20A%20look%20at%20InOrbit%20Control/Robot/live_button_on_timeline.png?width=300&amp;name=live_button_on_timeline.png 300w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20A%20look%20at%20InOrbit%20Control/Robot/live_button_on_timeline.png?width=600&amp;name=live_button_on_timeline.png 600w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20A%20look%20at%20InOrbit%20Control/Robot/live_button_on_timeline.png?width=900&amp;name=live_button_on_timeline.png 900w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20A%20look%20at%20InOrbit%20Control/Robot/live_button_on_timeline.png?width=1200&amp;name=live_button_on_timeline.png 1200w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20A%20look%20at%20InOrbit%20Control/Robot/live_button_on_timeline.png?width=1500&amp;name=live_button_on_timeline.png 1500w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20A%20look%20at%20InOrbit%20Control/Robot/live_button_on_timeline.png?width=1800&amp;name=live_button_on_timeline.png 1800w"
sizes="(max-width: 600px) 100vw, 600px" width="600"
alt="live_button_on_timeline" /></span>

This is the timeline widget with the “Live” switch off:

<span style="font-size: 11px;"><img
src="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20A%20look%20at%20InOrbit%20Control/Robot/live_button_off_timeline.png?width=600&amp;name=live_button_off_timeline.png"
style="width: 600px;" loading="lazy"
srcset="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20A%20look%20at%20InOrbit%20Control/Robot/live_button_off_timeline.png?width=300&amp;name=live_button_off_timeline.png 300w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20A%20look%20at%20InOrbit%20Control/Robot/live_button_off_timeline.png?width=600&amp;name=live_button_off_timeline.png 600w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20A%20look%20at%20InOrbit%20Control/Robot/live_button_off_timeline.png?width=900&amp;name=live_button_off_timeline.png 900w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20A%20look%20at%20InOrbit%20Control/Robot/live_button_off_timeline.png?width=1200&amp;name=live_button_off_timeline.png 1200w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20A%20look%20at%20InOrbit%20Control/Robot/live_button_off_timeline.png?width=1500&amp;name=live_button_off_timeline.png 1500w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20A%20look%20at%20InOrbit%20Control/Robot/live_button_off_timeline.png?width=1800&amp;name=live_button_off_timeline.png 1800w"
sizes="(max-width: 600px) 100vw, 600px" width="600"
alt="live_button_off_timeline" /></span>

This option is included in the following widgets:

- Timeline
- Incident Timeline
- KPI indicators
- Audit Log
- Key/Value sources
- Map
- Vitals
- Modes and tags
- ROS Diagnostics
- Locations
- Fleet status
- Robot actions

Despite this button appearing in all the widgets mentioned above, it
only can be toggled in the Timeline, Incident Timeline and Audit Log
widgets.  

###### *<span style="color: #666666;">Navigation dashboard</span>*

The **Navigation dashboard** lets you monitor a robot via **Map** and
**Camera** views, as well as other key data that is relevant during
navigation. It’s easy to switch between robots by using the robot
selector in the upper left.  At the right side of the bar, you’ll find
the **Hi-rez** toggle button (optional feature you can request to add)
to temporarily improve the quality of the primary camera , the map
selector, the map/camera button to switch between featuring the map or
the primary camera, the full-screen button for a deeper experience, and
the settings button to configure your navigation preferences.

In addition to executing one-click actions, you can relocalize the robot
if it gets lost, or use teleoperation to assist the robot with
navigation.

**<span style="color: #000000;"><img
src="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20A%20look%20at%20InOrbit%20Control/Navigation/nav_overview_stretch.png?width=600&amp;name=nav_overview_stretch.png"
style="width: 600px;"
srcset="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20A%20look%20at%20InOrbit%20Control/Navigation/nav_overview_stretch.png?width=300&amp;name=nav_overview_stretch.png 300w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20A%20look%20at%20InOrbit%20Control/Navigation/nav_overview_stretch.png?width=600&amp;name=nav_overview_stretch.png 600w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20A%20look%20at%20InOrbit%20Control/Navigation/nav_overview_stretch.png?width=900&amp;name=nav_overview_stretch.png 900w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20A%20look%20at%20InOrbit%20Control/Navigation/nav_overview_stretch.png?width=1200&amp;name=nav_overview_stretch.png 1200w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20A%20look%20at%20InOrbit%20Control/Navigation/nav_overview_stretch.png?width=1500&amp;name=nav_overview_stretch.png 1500w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20A%20look%20at%20InOrbit%20Control/Navigation/nav_overview_stretch.png?width=1800&amp;name=nav_overview_stretch.png 1800w"
sizes="(max-width: 600px) 100vw, 600px" width="600"
alt="nav_overview_stretch" /></span>**

###### *<span style="color: #666666;">Expand to full-screen navigation</span>*

<span style="color: #000000;">Clicking on the
</span>**<span style="color: #00b9d6;"><img
src="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20A%20look%20at%20InOrbit%20Control/Navigation/full_screen_nav.png?width=30&amp;name=full_screen_nav.png"
style="width: 30px;"
srcset="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20A%20look%20at%20InOrbit%20Control/Navigation/full_screen_nav.png?width=15&amp;name=full_screen_nav.png 15w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20A%20look%20at%20InOrbit%20Control/Navigation/full_screen_nav.png?width=30&amp;name=full_screen_nav.png 30w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20A%20look%20at%20InOrbit%20Control/Navigation/full_screen_nav.png?width=45&amp;name=full_screen_nav.png 45w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20A%20look%20at%20InOrbit%20Control/Navigation/full_screen_nav.png?width=60&amp;name=full_screen_nav.png 60w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20A%20look%20at%20InOrbit%20Control/Navigation/full_screen_nav.png?width=75&amp;name=full_screen_nav.png 75w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20A%20look%20at%20InOrbit%20Control/Navigation/full_screen_nav.png?width=90&amp;name=full_screen_nav.png 90w"
sizes="(max-width: 30px) 100vw, 30px" width="30"
alt="full_screen_nav" /></span>**<span style="color: #000000;"> button
expands the robot navigation widgets
(</span>**<span style="color: #000000;">Map</span>**<span style="color: #000000;">,
</span>**<span style="color: #000000;">Camera</span>**<span style="color: #000000;">,
</span>**<span style="color: #000000;">Teleop</span>**<span style="color: #000000;">)
to maximize the view, giving you extra details to precisely help the
robot navigate.</span>

**<span style="color: #000000;"><img
src="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20A%20look%20at%20InOrbit%20Control/Navigation/nav_full_screen.png?width=600&amp;name=nav_full_screen.png"
style="width: 600px;"
srcset="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20A%20look%20at%20InOrbit%20Control/Navigation/nav_full_screen.png?width=300&amp;name=nav_full_screen.png 300w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20A%20look%20at%20InOrbit%20Control/Navigation/nav_full_screen.png?width=600&amp;name=nav_full_screen.png 600w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20A%20look%20at%20InOrbit%20Control/Navigation/nav_full_screen.png?width=900&amp;name=nav_full_screen.png 900w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20A%20look%20at%20InOrbit%20Control/Navigation/nav_full_screen.png?width=1200&amp;name=nav_full_screen.png 1200w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20A%20look%20at%20InOrbit%20Control/Navigation/nav_full_screen.png?width=1500&amp;name=nav_full_screen.png 1500w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20A%20look%20at%20InOrbit%20Control/Navigation/nav_full_screen.png?width=1800&amp;name=nav_full_screen.png 1800w"
sizes="(max-width: 600px) 100vw, 600px" width="600"
alt="nav_full_screen" /></span>**

###### *<span style="color: #666666;">Exit full-screen navigation</span>*

Clicking again on the full-screen view button takes you back to the
**Navigation dashboard**.

###### *<span style="color: #666666;">Navigation considerations for Free Edition</span>*

The Navigation Dashboard in Free Edition is a limited version relative
to the Standard Edition described above. It consists of a single map and
camera view, and does not include the  Hi-rez option or full-screen
navigation because it is intended only for monitoring the robot, without
any interaction.

<img
src="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/01.png?width=624&amp;name=01.png"

</div>

</div>

</div>

<div id="button-container-id-quick-start" class="hs-accordion__item"
aria-expanded="">

<span id="quick-start" class="anchor"></span>

Quick start <span class="hs-accordion__item-icon"></span>

<div role="main">

<div class="hs-accordion__item-content">

With only these simple steps, InOrbit gives you near near instant
results for managing your robot fleet:

1.  <a href="#starting-setup" rel=" noopener">Set up your InOrbit
    account.</a>
2.  <a href="#add-robot" rel=" noopener">Add a robot.</a>
3.  <a href="#view-vitals" rel=" noopener">View the robot's vital signs.</a>

</div>

</div>

</div>

<div id="button-container-id-starting-setup" class="hs-accordion__item"
aria-expanded="">

<span id="starting-setup" class="anchor"></span>

Starting setup <span class="hs-accordion__item-icon"></span>

<div role="main">

<div class="hs-accordion__item-content">

Follow these steps to get your robots InOrbit.

##### What you need

- Internet access
- The starting hyperlink from
  InOrbit, [https://control.inorbit.ai](https://control.inorbit.ai/)
- A Google account

##### Steps

1.  In your browser, go to [InOrbit
    Control](https://control.inorbit.ai/).
2.  Click **Sign in with Google**.
3.  In the displayed dialog, click the desired Google account name to
    use to sign in.
4.  On the **"Preparing for launch...**" page, enter the name of your
    company or keep the default.
5.  Click **Launch InOrbit**.

##### Results

One or more InOrbit Control Dashboards are ready for you to add robots
to your InOrbit fleet, configure settings, view metrics and KPIs, and
more.

</div>

</div>

</div>

<div id="button-container-id-add-robot" class="hs-accordion__item"
aria-expanded="">

<span id="add-robot" class="anchor"></span>

Add a robot to your fleet <span class="hs-accordion__item-icon"></span>

<div role="main">

<div class="hs-accordion__item-content">

Here’s how you add a new robot to your InOrbit fleet. The process
involves copying and running a command on your robot's command line to
install the InOrbit agent.

##### <span style="color: #666666;">What you need</span>

- Internet access from your robot
- Shell access on your robot
- <span style="color: #807575;">The Owner or Administrator role in
  InOrbit Control</span>

##### <span style="color: #666666;">Steps</span>

1.  Log in to [InOrbit Control](https://control.inorbit.ai/).
2.  Select the Fleet dashboard to find and click <img
    src="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Add%20a%20robot%20to%20your%20fleet/new_robot_button.png?width=79&amp;name=new_robot_button.png"
    style="width: 79px;"
    srcset="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Add%20a%20robot%20to%20your%20fleet/new_robot_button.png?width=40&amp;name=new_robot_button.png 40w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Add%20a%20robot%20to%20your%20fleet/new_robot_button.png?width=79&amp;name=new_robot_button.png 79w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Add%20a%20robot%20to%20your%20fleet/new_robot_button.png?width=119&amp;name=new_robot_button.png 119w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Add%20a%20robot%20to%20your%20fleet/new_robot_button.png?width=158&amp;name=new_robot_button.png 158w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Add%20a%20robot%20to%20your%20fleet/new_robot_button.png?width=198&amp;name=new_robot_button.png 198w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Add%20a%20robot%20to%20your%20fleet/new_robot_button.png?width=237&amp;name=new_robot_button.png 237w"
    sizes="(max-width: 79px) 100vw, 79px" width="79"
    alt="new_robot_button" />.
3.  Select and copy the displayed curl command.
4.  Log in to your robot's Linux shell.
5.  At the command line, paste the copied curl command.
6.  Hit enter to continue through the InOrbit agent installation
    process. See notes below.
7.  After the installation is complete, you are returned to the shell
    command line.

##### <span style="color: #666666;">Notes</span>

- You are prompted to enter your username and other other information
  needed by the installer.
- You are kept informed of the installation progress, including
  installation of software required by the InOrbit robot agent.
- You might be instructed to enter various commands, such as
  <span style="font-family: 'Courier New', Courier, monospace; font-size: 12px;">sudo</span>,
  as required by the installer.

##### <span style="color: #666666;">Results</span>

- The InOrbit agent is now installed on this robot.
- The robot's host name is displayed in the Fleet widget in InOrbit
  Control.
- InOrbit begins collecting data about your robot for you to examine on
  InOrbit Control.

Please
see <a href="/docs#advanced-agent-configuration" rel="noopener">Advanced
agent configuration</a> for further information on customizing the
agent.

</div>

</div>

</div>

<div id="button-container-id-view-vitals" class="hs-accordion__item"
aria-expanded="">

<span id="view-vitals" class="anchor"></span>

View robot vital signs <span class="hs-accordion__item-icon"></span>

<div role="main">

<div class="hs-accordion__item-content">

After you've added a robot, you can see its operational status and vital
signs directly on InOrbit Control.

##### <span style="font-size: 13px; color: #000000;">What you need</span>

- You need to have <a href="/docs#add-robot" style="color: #3b3fb1;"
  rel="noopener"><span>added a robot</span></a> to your fleet.

##### <span style="font-size: 13px; color: #000000;">Steps</span>

1.  In your browser, go to InOrbit Control at
    <a href="https://control.inorbit.ai/"
    style="color: #3b3fb1;"><span>https://control.inorbit.ai</span></a>.
2.  If prompted, login with your Google account.
3.  Select the Robot Dashboard.
4.  Select the robot you want to check out using the Robot Control
    widget.
5.  Look for the Vitals widget, which is one of the default widgets in
    this dashboard.

##### <span style="font-size: 13px; color: #000000;">Results</span>

InOrbit Control shows you at-a-glance details about your robot's
operational status:

<img
src="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20View%20robot%20vital%20signs/robot_vitals.png?width=400&amp;name=robot_vitals.png"
style="width: 400px;"
srcset="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20View%20robot%20vital%20signs/robot_vitals.png?width=200&amp;name=robot_vitals.png 200w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20View%20robot%20vital%20signs/robot_vitals.png?width=400&amp;name=robot_vitals.png 400w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20View%20robot%20vital%20signs/robot_vitals.png?width=600&amp;name=robot_vitals.png 600w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20View%20robot%20vital%20signs/robot_vitals.png?width=800&amp;name=robot_vitals.png 800w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20View%20robot%20vital%20signs/robot_vitals.png?width=1000&amp;name=robot_vitals.png 1000w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20View%20robot%20vital%20signs/robot_vitals.png?width=1200&amp;name=robot_vitals.png 1200w"
sizes="(max-width: 400px) 100vw, 400px" width="400"
alt="robot_vitals" />

Explore other valuable operational details about your robot, including
selected data on a timeline, current mode, incident list, and more.

</div>

</div>

</div>

<div id="button-container-id-invite-user" class="hs-accordion__item"
aria-expanded="">

<span id="invite-user" class="anchor"></span>

Invite a user to your account
<span class="hs-accordion__item-icon"></span>

<div role="main">

<div class="hs-accordion__item-content">

Here’s how you invite a new user to your InOrbit account. This provides
the authentication to enable colleagues to have individual access to a
shared InOrbit account.

##### What you need

- An active InOrbit account
- Owner privileges on the account
- Email addresses for any users you want to invite

##### Steps

##### <img
src="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/invite-users.png?width=600&amp;name=invite-users.png"
style="width: 600px; display: block; margin: 0px auto;"
srcset="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/invite-users.png?width=300&amp;name=invite-users.png 300w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/invite-users.png?width=600&amp;name=invite-users.png 600w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/invite-users.png?width=900&amp;name=invite-users.png 900w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/invite-users.png?width=1200&amp;name=invite-users.png 1200w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/invite-users.png?width=1500&amp;name=invite-users.png 1500w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/invite-users.png?width=1800&amp;name=invite-users.png 1800w"
sizes="(max-width: 600px) 100vw, 600px" width="600"
alt="invite-users" />

1.  Log in to [InOrbit Control](https://control.inorbit.ai/).
2.  Go to Settings-\>Admin-\>Company Details-\>People.
3.  Click the circled + sign.
4.  Enter 1 or more email addresses separated by commas or spaces.
5.  Select the role (e.g., Owner, Administrator, User) that the new
    user(s) will be granted.
6.  Click SEND INVITES.
7.  User(s) should receive an email to confirm and will be added after
    confirming.
8.  After confirming, users can log in to [InOrbit
    Control](https://control.inorbit.ai/) with the privileges for that
    role.

##### Notes

- Roles can be configurable, but Owner is the only default role
  authorized to invite users.
- Invited users will appear in a Pending state in the People list until
  they have confirmed.
- While a user is Pending, a login link is available on their entry that
  can be copied and directly shared with them as a backup.

##### Results

- The invited users have individual logins to the shared account.
- The invited users start with their assigned role, which can be
  modified by account Owners.

</div>

</div>

</div>

</div>

</div>

</div>

</div>

</div>

</div>

</div>

</div>
