##### <span style="font-size: 13px; color: #666666;">About on-robot shell scripts</span>

On-robot shell scripts you can run after an incident are Linux scripts
that you yourself write and are completely under your control.

As an example, you might write a script that lists the top 10 running
processes on the robot and saves the list to a file. This could
potentially be useful for debugging a robot with high CPU utilization.
Such a script could look like the following:  
<span style="font-family: 'courier new', courier;">`# !/usr/bin/bash`  
`# where to save the script output`  
`export SAVEFILE="/tmp/top10cpu.txt"`  
`# list top 10 processes' percentage of CPU, PID, username,`  
`# and running program with arguments, sorted by percentage of CPU,`  
`# and saved to a file`  
`ps -eo pcpu,pid,user,args | sort -k1 -r -n | head -10 \`  
`> $SAVEFILE`</span>

- Store the script in \${HOME}/.inorbit/local/user_scripts.
- Make note of the name of the script on the robot because you need to
  specify the name when you define the action.

<span style="font-family: 'courier new', courier;"></span>

</div>

</div>

<div role="main">

<div class="hs-accordion__item-content">

##### <span style="font-size: 12px; color: #666666;">Collections</span>

As your fleet grows, InOrbit allows you to organize your robots
according to different criteria, such as hardware iteration, customer
deployment or location. This allows you to filter or group robots in
InOrbit Control based on these different criteria.

Each grouping of robots is called a ***collection***; each robot may be
part of one or more **collections**. All robots are members of the
default collection called **Fleet**.

You can customize the **collections** and robot membership in each
**collection** by going to **Settings \> Organization** in InOrbit
Control.<span style="font-size: 12px; color: #666666;"></span>

  

##### <span style="color: #666666;">Modes</span>

***Modes*** is a special **collection** type that can be used to
indicate the state or mode in which a robot is operating (e.g. Charging,
Idle, or Mission). This value can change dynamically and is assumed to
be MECE (Mutually Exclusive and Collectively Exhaustive), i.e. each
robot can be in one and only one mode at any given time.

Modes map to a color in the block below the robot name in the Fleet
Status widget.

<img
src="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Collections,%20Locations,%20Modes/mode.png?width=600&amp;name=mode.png"
style="width: 600px;"
srcset="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Collections%2C%20Locations%2C%20Modes/mode.png?width=300&amp;name=mode.png 300w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Collections%2C%20Locations%2C%20Modes/mode.png?width=600&amp;name=mode.png 600w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Collections%2C%20Locations%2C%20Modes/mode.png?width=900&amp;name=mode.png 900w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Collections%2C%20Locations%2C%20Modes/mode.png?width=1200&amp;name=mode.png 1200w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Collections%2C%20Locations%2C%20Modes/mode.png?width=1500&amp;name=mode.png 1500w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Collections%2C%20Locations%2C%20Modes/mode.png?width=1800&amp;name=mode.png 1800w"
sizes="(max-width: 600px) 100vw, 600px" width="600" alt="mode" />

You can group or filter the robots by **mode** in the Fleet dashboard
using the **Options** control.

<img
src="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Collections,%20Locations,%20Modes/fleet_modes_filter.png?width=600&amp;name=fleet_modes_filter.png"
style="width: 600px;"
srcset="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Collections%2C%20Locations%2C%20Modes/fleet_modes_filter.png?width=300&amp;name=fleet_modes_filter.png 300w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Collections%2C%20Locations%2C%20Modes/fleet_modes_filter.png?width=600&amp;name=fleet_modes_filter.png 600w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Collections%2C%20Locations%2C%20Modes/fleet_modes_filter.png?width=900&amp;name=fleet_modes_filter.png 900w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Collections%2C%20Locations%2C%20Modes/fleet_modes_filter.png?width=1200&amp;name=fleet_modes_filter.png 1200w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Collections%2C%20Locations%2C%20Modes/fleet_modes_filter.png?width=1500&amp;name=fleet_modes_filter.png 1500w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Collections%2C%20Locations%2C%20Modes/fleet_modes_filter.png?width=1800&amp;name=fleet_modes_filter.png 1800w"
sizes="(max-width: 600px) 100vw, 600px" width="600"
alt="fleet_modes_filter" />

Modes are reported in a custom data source. It is up to your application
code to report these modes; any modes reported in the data source that
do not match the modes defined here will be reported as Other.

###### *<span style="color: #666666;">Configure modes for your account</span>*

1.  Go to **Settings** \> **Organization** \> **Modes.**
2.  Select the data source that will determine the mode a robot is in
    (e.g., Mission Status):  
    <img
    src="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/12.png?width=600&amp;name=12.png"
    style="width: 600px;"
    srcset="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/12.png?width=300&amp;name=12.png 300w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/12.png?width=600&amp;name=12.png 600w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/12.png?width=900&amp;name=12.png 900w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/12.png?width=1200&amp;name=12.png 1200w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/12.png?width=1500&amp;name=12.png 1500w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/12.png?width=1800&amp;name=12.png 1800w"
    sizes="(max-width: 600px) 100vw, 600px" width="600" alt="12" />
3.  Now start adding the different values a mode might take. Click the
    ‘**+**’ button and add these one by one  
      
    <img
    src="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/13.png?width=600&amp;name=13.png"
    style="width: 600px;"
    srcset="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/13.png?width=300&amp;name=13.png 300w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/13.png?width=600&amp;name=13.png 600w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/13.png?width=900&amp;name=13.png 900w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/13.png?width=1200&amp;name=13.png 1200w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/13.png?width=1500&amp;name=13.png 1500w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/13.png?width=1800&amp;name=13.png 1800w"
    sizes="(max-width: 600px) 100vw, 600px" width="600" alt="13" />  
      
    Note: The **tag** field is the visible name the mode will have,
    which doesn’t need to match the actual data source’s values for the
    mode. Tags are unique.  
      
    If values are not specified, it is assumed that the tag matches the
    value that the data source will take. So if,
    <span style="font-size: 12px; color: #434343; background-color: #efefef;">Mission
    Status </span>can be any of
    <span style="font-size: 12px; color: #434343; background-color: #efefef;">Mission</span>,
    <span style="font-size: 12px; color: #434343; background-color: #efefef;">Idle</span>,
    <span style="font-size: 12px; color: #434343; background-color: #efefef;">Charging</span>,
    <span style="font-size: 12px; color: #434343; background-color: #efefef;">Inventory</span>
    or
    <span style="font-size: 12px; color: #434343; background-color: #efefef;">Error</span>,
    the configuration should look like this  
      
    <img
    src="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/14.png?width=450&amp;name=14.png"
    style="width: 450px;"
    srcset="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/14.png?width=225&amp;name=14.png 225w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/14.png?width=450&amp;name=14.png 450w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/14.png?width=675&amp;name=14.png 675w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/14.png?width=900&amp;name=14.png 900w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/14.png?width=1125&amp;name=14.png 1125w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/14.png?width=1350&amp;name=14.png 1350w"
    sizes="(max-width: 450px) 100vw, 450px" width="450" alt="14" />  
      
    You can also change the order of modes to match the robot behavior;
    for example, if your robots usually change from Charging to Idle to
    Mission and back to Charging, you may organize them in that order.
    Colors are automatically assigned to each mode.  
      
    If, for example, the
    <span style="font-size: 12px; color: #434343; background-color: #efefef;">Mission</span>
    mode is triggered when
    <span style="font-size: 12px; color: #434343; background-color: #efefef;">Mission
    Status </span>matches any of a set of values (e.g.,
    <span style="font-size: 12px; color: #434343; background-color: #efefef;">mission</span>,
    <span style="font-size: 12px; color: #434343; background-color: #efefef;">in_mission</span>,
    <span style="font-size: 12px; color: #434343; background-color: #efefef;">running</span>)
    or a value different from the tag, you can specify these using the
    <span style="font-size: 12px; color: #434343; background-color: #efefef;">values</span>
    field:  
      
    <img
    src="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/15.png?width=600&amp;name=15.png"
    style="width: 600px;"
    srcset="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/15.png?width=300&amp;name=15.png 300w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/15.png?width=600&amp;name=15.png 600w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/15.png?width=900&amp;name=15.png 900w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/15.png?width=1200&amp;name=15.png 1200w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/15.png?width=1500&amp;name=15.png 1500w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/15.png?width=1800&amp;name=15.png 1800w"
    sizes="(max-width: 600px) 100vw, 600px" width="600" alt="15" />  
      
4.  Done! Now you’ll just need the robot to publish
    <span style="font-size: 12px; color: #434343; background-color: #efefef;">Mission
    Status=\<status\></span> and if the mission status is any of the
    values defined above, the robot will appear in that mode
5.  If the status is not on that list, the mode will be “**Other**”. For
    example:  
      
    <img
    src="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Collections,%20Locations,%20Modes/modes_other.png?width=600&amp;name=modes_other.png"
    style="width: 600px;"
    srcset="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Collections%2C%20Locations%2C%20Modes/modes_other.png?width=300&amp;name=modes_other.png 300w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Collections%2C%20Locations%2C%20Modes/modes_other.png?width=600&amp;name=modes_other.png 600w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Collections%2C%20Locations%2C%20Modes/modes_other.png?width=900&amp;name=modes_other.png 900w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Collections%2C%20Locations%2C%20Modes/modes_other.png?width=1200&amp;name=modes_other.png 1200w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Collections%2C%20Locations%2C%20Modes/modes_other.png?width=1500&amp;name=modes_other.png 1500w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Collections%2C%20Locations%2C%20Modes/modes_other.png?width=1800&amp;name=modes_other.png 1800w"
    sizes="(max-width: 600px) 100vw, 600px" width="600" alt="modes_other" />

##### <span style="color: #666666;">Regular collections</span>

All other collections can be defined in the **Collections** tab in
**Settings** \> **Organization**. You can add your own collection by
clicking the circled + sign. Choose a type such as **Customer** or
**Hardware**.

Enter a unique name, and optionally add notes and check the box to
automatically add new robots to that collection

Depending on the nature of the data source, **collections** can be
either ***static*** or ***dynamic***.

###### *<span style="color: #666666;">Static</span>*

**Static** collections have fixed tags, manually generated, as the
members are solely based on tags rather than data sources. Robots need
to be explicitly assigned to a static collection by applying a
particular tag to it. By default, several collections, including
**Deployment**, **Hardware**, **Customer** and **Other**, are static.

###### *<span style="color: #666666;">Configure static collections for your account</span>*

1.  Go to **Settings** \> **Organization** \> **Collections**
2.  Click on the circled **+** sign, and a new row will be displayed for
    you to add a new collection.  
    <img
    src="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/16.png?width=600&amp;name=16.png"
    style="width: 600px;"
    srcset="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/16.png?width=300&amp;name=16.png 300w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/16.png?width=600&amp;name=16.png 600w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/16.png?width=900&amp;name=16.png 900w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/16.png?width=1200&amp;name=16.png 1200w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/16.png?width=1500&amp;name=16.png 1500w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/16.png?width=1800&amp;name=16.png 1800w"
    sizes="(max-width: 600px) 100vw, 600px" width="600" alt="16" />
3.  Now you’ll need to create **Tags** for this collection.
    1.  Go to **Settings** \> **Organization** \> **Tags**
    2.  Select the tab for the **Collection** for which you would like
        to add a tag, and click on the circled **+** sign. This will
        present a new row where you can configure the new tab  
          
        <img
        src="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/17.png?width=600&amp;name=17.png"
        style="width: 600px;"
        srcset="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/17.png?width=300&amp;name=17.png 300w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/17.png?width=600&amp;name=17.png 600w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/17.png?width=900&amp;name=17.png 900w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/17.png?width=1200&amp;name=17.png 1200w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/17.png?width=1500&amp;name=17.png 1500w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/17.png?width=1800&amp;name=17.png 1800w"
        sizes="(max-width: 600px) 100vw, 600px" width="600" alt="17" />
    3.  Enter the **tag** name, which should be unique  
          
        <img
        src="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/18.png?width=600&amp;name=18.png"
        style="width: 600px;"
        srcset="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/18.png?width=300&amp;name=18.png 300w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/18.png?width=600&amp;name=18.png 600w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/18.png?width=900&amp;name=18.png 900w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/18.png?width=1200&amp;name=18.png 1200w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/18.png?width=1500&amp;name=18.png 1500w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/18.png?width=1800&amp;name=18.png 1800w"
        sizes="(max-width: 600px) 100vw, 600px" width="600" alt="18" />
    4.  Now use the **Robots** tab in **Settings** \> **Organization**
        and assign robots to this tag. For example, If you want to
        update tags for a robot named ‘Kepler’, you’ll need to click on
        the dropdown next to the robot name and select the tags it
        belongs to.  
          
        <img
        src="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/19.png?width=600&amp;name=19.png"
        style="width: 600px;"
        srcset="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/19.png?width=300&amp;name=19.png 300w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/19.png?width=600&amp;name=19.png 600w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/19.png?width=900&amp;name=19.png 900w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/19.png?width=1200&amp;name=19.png 1200w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/19.png?width=1500&amp;name=19.png 1500w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/19.png?width=1800&amp;name=19.png 1800w"
        sizes="(max-width: 600px) 100vw, 600px" width="600" alt="19" />  
          
    5.  Then for the rest of the tags you can simply select the existing
        collection tab and add a new tag for it. Note that if the data
        source takes a value which doesn’t have a tag defined, a robot
        going into that state will have the tag “**Unassigned**”.

###### *<span style="color: #666666;">Dynamic</span>*

***Dynamic*** *collections* are collections that are based on known data
sources and thus membership can change as values change in the selected
underlying data source. These are useful for enabling robots to be
filtered or grouped by attributes that reflect the current state of that
robot, such as the software version. **Modes** are an example of a
dynamic collection that is built into the InOrbit platform.

###### *<span style="color: #666666;">Configure dynamic collections for your account</span>*

1.  Go to **Settings** \> **Organization** \> **Collections**
2.  Click on the circled **+** sign, and a new row will be displayed for
    you to add a new collection  
      
    <img
    src="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/20.png?width=600&amp;name=20.png"
    style="width: 600px;"
    srcset="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/20.png?width=300&amp;name=20.png 300w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/20.png?width=600&amp;name=20.png 600w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/20.png?width=900&amp;name=20.png 900w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/20.png?width=1200&amp;name=20.png 1200w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/20.png?width=1500&amp;name=20.png 1500w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/20.png?width=1800&amp;name=20.png 1800w"
    sizes="(max-width: 600px) 100vw, 600px" width="600" alt="20" />
3.  Give this new collection a **name** (e.g., Software Version).
4.  You will see a new field on the right, which is the **data source**
    selector. The value of this data source on a robot will determine
    its associated tag.  
    <img
    src="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/21.png?width=500&amp;name=21.png"
    style="width: 500px;"
    srcset="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/21.png?width=250&amp;name=21.png 250w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/21.png?width=500&amp;name=21.png 500w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/21.png?width=750&amp;name=21.png 750w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/21.png?width=1000&amp;name=21.png 1000w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/21.png?width=1250&amp;name=21.png 1250w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/21.png?width=1500&amp;name=21.png 1500w"
    sizes="(max-width: 500px) 100vw, 500px" width="500" alt="21" />
5.  Now you’ll need to create **Tags** for this collection. You can
    either do it:
    1.  Manually as described above for **Static Collections**.
    2.  Automatically
    3.  Click the **Auto create tags** checkbox on the **Collections**
        config. This will automatically create new tags based on the
        values that the associated data source takes, which will be
        reflected in the **Tags** section.  
        <img
        src="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/22.png?width=600&amp;name=22.png"
        style="width: 600px;"
        srcset="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/22.png?width=300&amp;name=22.png 300w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/22.png?width=600&amp;name=22.png 600w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/22.png?width=900&amp;name=22.png 900w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/22.png?width=1200&amp;name=22.png 1200w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/22.png?width=1500&amp;name=22.png 1500w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/22.png?width=1800&amp;name=22.png 1800w"
        sizes="(max-width: 600px) 100vw, 600px" width="600" alt="22" />  
          
        **Note**: clicking on the robot count for a tag will redirect
        you to Control, with the fleet status filter set to the specific
        tag, so only those robots will appear.

You can edit an already created collection (change its name, switch
between static and dynamic, update a data source for dynamic
collections) by clicking on a row within the **Collections** tab in
**Settings** \> **Organization**. This will enable edit mode.  
  

You can also delete **collections** or **tags** with the trash button.
To delete a **collection**, you must first delete all its **tags**. To
delete a **tag**, first make sure there are no robots on it.

in the **Fleet dashboard**. For example:

<div class="hs-accordion__item-content">

As an Owner or Administrator, login to
<a href="https://control.inorbit.ai/"
style="color: #3b3fb1;"><span>InOrbit Control</span></a>. Click on the
<img
src="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/23.png?width=10&amp;name=23.png"
style="width: 10px;"
srcset="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/23.png?width=5&amp;name=23.png 5w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/23.png?width=10&amp;name=23.png 10w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/23.png?width=15&amp;name=23.png 15w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/23.png?width=20&amp;name=23.png 20w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/23.png?width=25&amp;name=23.png 25w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/23.png?width=30&amp;name=23.png 30w"

upper right and select **Settings,** where you can set up your

###### *<span style="color: #666666;"> </span><span style="color: #666666;">Create a new dashboard</span>*

Navigate to the Dashboards section under the Robot Data tab. This area
lets you edit and create **dashboards**.

<img
src="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/24.png?width=650&amp;name=24.png"

<img

Any dashboard can be renamed by typing the new name into the “dashboard
title” field.

###### *<span style="color: #666666;">Change visibility of each dashboard</span>*

Visibility of **dashboards** lets a company limit access to specific
capabilities of **InOrbit Control**. Creating specific **dashboards**
intended for different roles or tasks can avoid screen clutter, letting
the user focus on their responsibilities. By default, **dashboards** are
hidden from all roles. Make sure to select roles that should have
visibility of the new dashboard.

In the example below, the **Executive dashboard** is set to be visible
to **Owner**, **Administrator, Manager**, **Engineer** and **Operator**
**roles**, but not visible to people with the **User role**.

<span style="font-size: 10px; color: #172b4d;"><img
src="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/27.png?width=650&amp;name=27.png"
style="width: 650px;"
srcset="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/27.png?width=325&amp;name=27.png 325w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/27.png?width=650&amp;name=27.png 650w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/27.png?width=975&amp;name=27.png 975w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/27.png?width=1300&amp;name=27.png 1300w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/27.png?width=1625&amp;name=27.png 1625w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/27.png?width=1950&amp;name=27.png 1950w"
sizes="(max-width: 650px) 100vw, 650px" width="650" alt="27" /></span>

In our example New Dashboard, we are going to set visibility to
**Operator** only. 

<img
src="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/28.png?width=650&amp;name=28.png"
style="width: 650px;"
srcset="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/28.png?width=325&amp;name=28.png 325w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/28.png?width=650&amp;name=28.png 650w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/28.png?width=975&amp;name=28.png 975w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/28.png?width=1300&amp;name=28.png 1300w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/28.png?width=1625&amp;name=28.png 1625w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/28.png?width=1950&amp;name=28.png 1950w"
sizes="(max-width: 650px) 100vw, 650px" width="650" alt="28" />

###### *<span style="color: #666666;">Add a section in a dashboard</span>*

**Dashboards** consist of **sections** and **widgets**. **Sections** are
like named folders that can contain a set of **widgets**.

Click on the<img
src="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/29.png?width=22&amp;name=29.png"
style="width: 22px;"
srcset="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/29.png?width=11&amp;name=29.png 11w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/29.png?width=22&amp;name=29.png 22w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/29.png?width=33&amp;name=29.png 33w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/29.png?width=44&amp;name=29.png 44w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/29.png?width=55&amp;name=29.png 55w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/29.png?width=66&amp;name=29.png 66w"
sizes="(max-width: 22px) 100vw, 22px" width="22" alt="29" /> button to
create a new section in this **dashboard**.

**<img
src="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/30.png?width=600&amp;name=30.png"
style="width: 600px;"

Click the **section** **scope** dropdown menu and select Fleet or Robot,

###### *<span style="color: #666666;">What are the control widgets?</span>*

There are 2 control **widgets** available: **Fleet Control** and **Robot
Control**. These should definitely be added to any new dashboards where
you want to be able to select within the fleet or focus on a particular
robot.

**Fleet Control** lets you filter your robot fleet by a collection (tag)
and/or an attribute status. It also allows grouping robots by a
**collection**, or sorting by status or alphabetical order.

<img
src="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Create%20and%20edit%20dashboards/fleet_bar-1.png?width=600&amp;name=fleet_bar-1.png"
style="width: 600px;"
srcset="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Create%20and%20edit%20dashboards/fleet_bar-1.png?width=300&amp;name=fleet_bar-1.png 300w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Create%20and%20edit%20dashboards/fleet_bar-1.png?width=600&amp;name=fleet_bar-1.png 600w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Create%20and%20edit%20dashboards/fleet_bar-1.png?width=900&amp;name=fleet_bar-1.png 900w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Create%20and%20edit%20dashboards/fleet_bar-1.png?width=1200&amp;name=fleet_bar-1.png 1200w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Create%20and%20edit%20dashboards/fleet_bar-1.png?width=1500&amp;name=fleet_bar-1.png 1500w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Create%20and%20edit%20dashboards/fleet_bar-1.png?width=1800&amp;name=fleet_bar-1.png 1800w"
sizes="(max-width: 600px) 100vw, 600px" width="600" alt="fleet_bar-1" />

**Robot Control** lets you search for a robot that you have access to
and set it as your selected robot to monitor. The widget also has handy
shortcuts, such as a button to restart the InOrbit **agent,** or another
that opens the **Navigation** dashboard.

<img
src="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Create%20and%20edit%20dashboards/robot_bar.png?width=600&amp;name=robot_bar.png"
style="width: 600px;"
srcset="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Create%20and%20edit%20dashboards/robot_bar.png?width=300&amp;name=robot_bar.png 300w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Create%20and%20edit%20dashboards/robot_bar.png?width=600&amp;name=robot_bar.png 600w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Create%20and%20edit%20dashboards/robot_bar.png?width=900&amp;name=robot_bar.png 900w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Create%20and%20edit%20dashboards/robot_bar.png?width=1200&amp;name=robot_bar.png 1200w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Create%20and%20edit%20dashboards/robot_bar.png?width=1500&amp;name=robot_bar.png 1500w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Create%20and%20edit%20dashboards/robot_bar.png?width=1800&amp;name=robot_bar.png 1800w"
sizes="(max-width: 600px) 100vw, 600px" width="600" alt="robot_bar" />

 

</div>

</div>

<div role="main">

<div class="hs-accordion__item-content">

<span style="color: #222222;">InOrbit’s agent can subscribe to ROS image
topics (with messages of type
</span>[Image](http://docs.ros.org/en/noetic/api/sensor_msgs/html/msg/Image.html)<span style="color: #222222;">
and
</span>[CompressedImage](https://docs.ros.org/en/api/sensor_msgs/html/msg/CompressedImage.html)<span style="color: #222222;">)
and send samples of those images to the cloud.
</span><span style="color: #222222;">The images are compressed as JPEG,
throttled and down-sampled. </span>

<span style="color: #222222;">This image snapshot based approach is
designed to provide the user enough information about the robot’s
environment when monitoring or operating it, while minimizing CPU and
network usage on the robot. There is a tradeoff between image quality,
size and frame rate, as we’ll see in the
</span>[configuration](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1_iGLr_uKSW_chB8XL9EnoYahUHDn5wOwvcLUAr3v_4U/edit#heading=h.juk8wgb76m1p)<span style="color: #222222;">
section.</span>

<span style="color: #222222;">Video encoding and streaming is available
as a separate offering. Please contact
</span>[support@inorbit.ai](mailto:support@inorbit.ai)<span style="color: #222222;">
for more information.</span>

## <span style="font-size: 16px; color: #000000;">Configuration</span>

<span style="color: #222222;">If you go to Settings \> Navigation \>
Cameras, you’ll find a ‘Configuration preset’ dropdown with 3
options:</span>

<span style="color: #222222;"><img
src="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/33.png?width=600&amp;name=33.png"
style="width: 600px;" loading="lazy"
srcset="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/33.png?width=300&amp;name=33.png 300w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/33.png?width=600&amp;name=33.png 600w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/33.png?width=900&amp;name=33.png 900w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/33.png?width=1200&amp;name=33.png 1200w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/33.png?width=1500&amp;name=33.png 1500w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/33.png?width=1800&amp;name=33.png 1800w"
sizes="(max-width: 600px) 100vw, 600px" width="600" alt="33" /></span>

<span style="color: #222222;">Each preset represent a different
configuration for processing and transmitting camera images from the
agent to the cloud:</span>

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|   | **<span style="color: #222222;">Higher Frequency</span>** | **<span style="color: #222222;">Bandwidth Intensive</span>** | **<span style="color: #222222;">Higher Quality</span>** |
| <span style="color: #222222;">Encoding</span> | <span style="color: #222222;">mono</span> | <span style="color: #222222;">RGB</span> | <span style="color: #222222;">RGB</span> |
| [JPEG compression quality](https://docs.opencv.org/3.4/d8/d6a/group__imgcodecs__flags.html#ga292d81be8d76901bff7988d18d2b42ac) | <span style="color: #222222;">10</span> | <span style="color: #222222;">15</span> | <span style="color: #222222;">30</span> |
| <span style="color: #222222;">Size</span> | <span style="color: #222222;">380x240</span> | <span style="color: #222222;">380x240</span> | <span style="color: #222222;">720x380</span> |
| <span style="color: #222222;">Frame Rate \[Hz\]</span> | <span style="color: #222222;">2</span> | <span style="color: #222222;">1</span> | <span style="color: #222222;">0.2</span> |
| <span style="color: #222222;">Message size \[kB\]</span> | <span style="color: #222222;">3</span> | <span style="color: #222222;">4.5</span> | <span style="color: #222222;">12</span> |

 

<span style="color: #222222;">These are the combinations we offer in
terms of quality / size / frame rate balance, though we'd recommend
using 'Higher Frequency' to preserve bandwidth if you don't need a high
definition. With the default account set-up, if you make resolution and
frequency too high, you may start to experience lag (latency), which is
not a desired effect.</span>

## <span style="font-size: 16px; color: #000000;">Customizing camera views</span>

<span style="color: #000000;">If you need to focus on the images from a
given camera widget, you can enlarge it by clicking within it:</span>

<span style="color: #000000;"><img
src="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/34.png?width=300&amp;name=34.png"
style="width: 300px;" loading="lazy"
srcset="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/34.png?width=150&amp;name=34.png 150w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/34.png?width=300&amp;name=34.png 300w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/34.png?width=450&amp;name=34.png 450w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/34.png?width=600&amp;name=34.png 600w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/34.png?width=750&amp;name=34.png 750w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/34.png?width=900&amp;name=34.png 900w"
sizes="(max-width: 300px) 100vw, 300px" width="300" alt="34" /></span>

<span style="color: #000000;">You can further customize the view of a
camera widget by clicking on the camera label (‘C’, ‘L’ or ‘R’ in the
example below). The label acts like a switch that allows you to
alternate between: </span>

- <span style="color: #000000;">zoom images</span>
- <span style="color: #000000;">turn off a camera</span>
- <span style="color: #000000;">full images</span>

<span style="color: #000000;">Turning cameras off is a useful way to
save resources during regular operations, when camera images are not
strictly needed.</span>

<span style="color: #000000;"><img
src="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/36.png?width=349&amp;name=36.png"
style="width: 349px;" loading="lazy"
srcset="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/36.png?width=175&amp;name=36.png 175w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/36.png?width=349&amp;name=36.png 349w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/36.png?width=524&amp;name=36.png 524w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/36.png?width=698&amp;name=36.png 698w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/36.png?width=873&amp;name=36.png 873w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/36.png?width=1047&amp;name=36.png 1047w"
sizes="(max-width: 349px) 100vw, 349px" width="349" alt="36" /></span>

<span style="color: #000000;">If you need to re-enable a camera for a
moment, then you can easily turn it back on by clicking on the
label.</span>

<span style="color: #000000;">Please note that, in order to preserve
bandwidth, InOrbit’s agent sends images to the cloud only if a user from
a company (at least one) has a Navigation Dashboard open and the camera
on. </span>

## <span style="font-size: 16px; color: #000000;">High-resolution mode</span>

<span style="color: #222222;">InOrbit offers an advanced option to
display the featured camera in an alternative resolution for  a limited
time (it currently times out after 1 minute), using the Hi-rez switch.
This enables you to deeply optimize bandwidth usage by only transmitting
full quality images when an active operator needs that quality to
discern a detail, such as an aisle or zone number. </span>

<span style="color: #222222;">The Hi-Rez mode provides high quality
images, achieving the goal of being able to see details for a brief
period of time. The update frequency is trimmed down to 0.1 Hz to
preserve bandwidth. Just click the Hi-rez switch to toggle it
on/off.</span>

<span style="font-size: 12px; color: #807575;"><img
src="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/37.png?width=624&amp;name=37.png"
style="width: 624px;" loading="lazy"

## <span style="font-size: 16px; color: #000000;">Advanced settings</span>

<span style="color: #222222;">Besides the default presets, it is
possible to select any arbitrary configuration that you prefer (for both
the regular image snapshots and Hi-rez mode). For that, you will need to
provide the following parameters to our </span>[Customer Success
team](mailto:success@inorbit.ai)<span style="color: #222222;">:</span>

- <span style="color: #222222;">Encoding (mono or rgb)</span>
- <span style="color: #222222;">JPEG compression quality</span>
- <span style="color: #222222;">Image size</span>
- <span style="color: #222222;">Frame rate \[Hz\]</span>

<span style="color: #222222;">After setting up that configuration,
cameras will start streaming with the desired resolution and frequency
that you choose, along with the preset options in the dropdown
menu.</span>

</div>

</div>

<div role="main">

<div class="hs-accordion__item-content">

When autonomy fails and other means of resolution fall short,
<a href="https://control.inorbit.ai/"
style="color: #3b3fb1;"><span>InOrbit Control</span></a> offers powerful
Intervention capabilities that allow you to temporarily take manual
control of your robots and teleoperate them back to safety and autonomy.

This ability gives the human operator using InOrbit a greater power over
the physical presence of the robot so it must be used with caution.

##### <span style="font-size: 13px; color: #000000;">Overview</span>

Relocalization and Teleoperation are available when opening the
Navigation screen for a particular robot. The controls for both
relocalization and teleoperation can be found on the right side of the
Navigation dashboard.

**<img
src="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/map_navigation_overview.png?width=600&amp;name=map_navigation_overview.png"
style="width: 600px;" loading="lazy"
srcset="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/map_navigation_overview.png?width=300&amp;name=map_navigation_overview.png 300w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/map_navigation_overview.png?width=600&amp;name=map_navigation_overview.png 600w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/map_navigation_overview.png?width=900&amp;name=map_navigation_overview.png 900w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/map_navigation_overview.png?width=1200&amp;name=map_navigation_overview.png 1200w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/map_navigation_overview.png?width=1500&amp;name=map_navigation_overview.png 1500w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/map_navigation_overview.png?width=1800&amp;name=map_navigation_overview.png 1800w"
sizes="(max-width: 600px) 100vw, 600px" width="600"
alt="map_navigation_overview" />**

InOrbit Control offers flexible layouts to display more detail across
maps and cameras. 

The “map/camera” button, between the map selector and the settings
button,  toggles between views that feature the map and the primary
selected camera.. 

Cameras are ordered based on the order of cameras in **Settings \>
Navigation \> Camera**. You can select the primary camera by clicking on
the image within any camera view. Similarly, you can feature the map by
clicking on the small map view when a camera is featured. You can turn
off a camera (good for reducing bandwidth) by clicking on the label in
the upper right of any camera view.

This is an example of featuring the primary camera.

<img
src="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Navigation%20Relocalization%20+%20Teleoperation/full_camera.png?width=600&amp;name=full_camera.png"
style="width: 600px;" loading="lazy"
srcset="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Navigation%20Relocalization%20+%20Teleoperation/full_camera.png?width=300&amp;name=full_camera.png 300w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Navigation%20Relocalization%20+%20Teleoperation/full_camera.png?width=600&amp;name=full_camera.png 600w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Navigation%20Relocalization%20+%20Teleoperation/full_camera.png?width=900&amp;name=full_camera.png 900w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Navigation%20Relocalization%20+%20Teleoperation/full_camera.png?width=1200&amp;name=full_camera.png 1200w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Navigation%20Relocalization%20+%20Teleoperation/full_camera.png?width=1500&amp;name=full_camera.png 1500w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Navigation%20Relocalization%20+%20Teleoperation/full_camera.png?width=1800&amp;name=full_camera.png 1800w"
sizes="(max-width: 600px) 100vw, 600px" width="600" alt="full_camera" />

To improve the video quality, InOrbit offers an advanced option to
display the featured camera in high resolution for  a limited time (it
currently times out after 1 minute), using the Hi-rez switch. This
enables you to deeply optimize bandwidth usage by only transmitting full
quality images when an active operator needs that quality to discern a
detail, such as an aisle or zone number. 

Updates will be significantly slower while in Hi-Rez mode based on the
additional data transmission, but the primary goal is to be able to see
details for a brief period of time. Just click the Hi-rez switch to
toggle it on/off.

<img
src="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Navigation%20Relocalization%20+%20Teleoperation/hi_res.png?width=600&amp;name=hi_res.png"
style="width: 600px;" loading="lazy"
srcset="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Navigation%20Relocalization%20+%20Teleoperation/hi_res.png?width=300&amp;name=hi_res.png 300w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Navigation%20Relocalization%20+%20Teleoperation/hi_res.png?width=600&amp;name=hi_res.png 600w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Navigation%20Relocalization%20+%20Teleoperation/hi_res.png?width=900&amp;name=hi_res.png 900w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Navigation%20Relocalization%20+%20Teleoperation/hi_res.png?width=1200&amp;name=hi_res.png 1200w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Navigation%20Relocalization%20+%20Teleoperation/hi_res.png?width=1500&amp;name=hi_res.png 1500w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Navigation%20Relocalization%20+%20Teleoperation/hi_res.png?width=1800&amp;name=hi_res.png 1800w"
sizes="(max-width: 600px) 100vw, 600px" width="600" alt="hi_res" />

##### <span style="font-size: 13px; color: #000000;">Relocalization</span>

To ensure that the robot is properly localized to accurately track and
control the robot navigation, the map displays lidar dots reflecting how
the robot is currently recognizing obstacles. If the lidar dots are not
properly oriented with features on the map, navigation becomes more
unreliable.

Relocalization may be all that is required to restore proper navigation
by the robot, or it may be the first step before taking over navigation
via teleoperation.

First activate relocalization by clicking the Relocalize button.

<img
src="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Navigation%20Relocalization%20+%20Teleoperation/navigation_map_reloc.png?width=600&amp;name=navigation_map_reloc.png"
style="width: 600px;" loading="lazy"
srcset="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Navigation%20Relocalization%20+%20Teleoperation/navigation_map_reloc.png?width=300&amp;name=navigation_map_reloc.png 300w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Navigation%20Relocalization%20+%20Teleoperation/navigation_map_reloc.png?width=600&amp;name=navigation_map_reloc.png 600w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Navigation%20Relocalization%20+%20Teleoperation/navigation_map_reloc.png?width=900&amp;name=navigation_map_reloc.png 900w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Navigation%20Relocalization%20+%20Teleoperation/navigation_map_reloc.png?width=1200&amp;name=navigation_map_reloc.png 1200w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Navigation%20Relocalization%20+%20Teleoperation/navigation_map_reloc.png?width=1500&amp;name=navigation_map_reloc.png 1500w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Navigation%20Relocalization%20+%20Teleoperation/navigation_map_reloc.png?width=1800&amp;name=navigation_map_reloc.png 1800w"
sizes="(max-width: 600px) 100vw, 600px" width="600"
alt="navigation_map_reloc" />

It is easy to relocalize a robot with two controls:

- Clicking and dragging the rotation control above the robot avatar
  enables rotating the map features  
    
  <img
  src="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Navigation%20Relocalization%20+%20Teleoperation/relocalize_rotate.png?width=600&amp;name=relocalize_rotate.png"
  style="width: 600px;" loading="lazy"
  srcset="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Navigation%20Relocalization%20+%20Teleoperation/relocalize_rotate.png?width=300&amp;name=relocalize_rotate.png 300w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Navigation%20Relocalization%20+%20Teleoperation/relocalize_rotate.png?width=600&amp;name=relocalize_rotate.png 600w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Navigation%20Relocalization%20+%20Teleoperation/relocalize_rotate.png?width=900&amp;name=relocalize_rotate.png 900w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Navigation%20Relocalization%20+%20Teleoperation/relocalize_rotate.png?width=1200&amp;name=relocalize_rotate.png 1200w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Navigation%20Relocalization%20+%20Teleoperation/relocalize_rotate.png?width=1500&amp;name=relocalize_rotate.png 1500w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Navigation%20Relocalization%20+%20Teleoperation/relocalize_rotate.png?width=1800&amp;name=relocalize_rotate.png 1800w"
  sizes="(max-width: 600px) 100vw, 600px" width="600"
  alt="relocalize_rotate" />
- Clicking and dragging on the robot avatar enables translating the map
  in xy space to align the lidar dots with the features  
    
  <img
  src="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Navigation%20Relocalization%20+%20Teleoperation/relocalize_move.png?width=600&amp;name=relocalize_move.png"
  style="width: 600px;" loading="lazy"
  srcset="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Navigation%20Relocalization%20+%20Teleoperation/relocalize_move.png?width=300&amp;name=relocalize_move.png 300w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Navigation%20Relocalization%20+%20Teleoperation/relocalize_move.png?width=600&amp;name=relocalize_move.png 600w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Navigation%20Relocalization%20+%20Teleoperation/relocalize_move.png?width=900&amp;name=relocalize_move.png 900w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Navigation%20Relocalization%20+%20Teleoperation/relocalize_move.png?width=1200&amp;name=relocalize_move.png 1200w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Navigation%20Relocalization%20+%20Teleoperation/relocalize_move.png?width=1500&amp;name=relocalize_move.png 1500w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Navigation%20Relocalization%20+%20Teleoperation/relocalize_move.png?width=1800&amp;name=relocalize_move.png 1800w"
  sizes="(max-width: 600px) 100vw, 600px" width="600"
  alt="relocalize_move" />

Click on the check mark in the lower right to confirm relocalization. If
you decide to cancel or start over, click the Relocalize button again to
abort the ongoing action.

<img
src="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Navigation%20Relocalization%20+%20Teleoperation/relocalize_confirm.png?width=400&amp;name=relocalize_confirm.png"
style="width: 400px;" loading="lazy"
srcset="https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Navigation%20Relocalization%20+%20Teleoperation/relocalize_confirm.png?width=200&amp;name=relocalize_confirm.png 200w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Navigation%20Relocalization%20+%20Teleoperation/relocalize_confirm.png?width=400&amp;name=relocalize_confirm.png 400w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Navigation%20Relocalization%20+%20Teleoperation/relocalize_confirm.png?width=600&amp;name=relocalize_confirm.png 600w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Navigation%20Relocalization%20+%20Teleoperation/relocalize_confirm.png?width=800&amp;name=relocalize_confirm.png 800w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Navigation%20Relocalization%20+%20Teleoperation/relocalize_confirm.png?width=1000&amp;name=relocalize_confirm.png 1000w, https://www.inorbit.ai/hs-fs/hubfs/doc/NDL%20Images/Docs/HOWTO%20Navigation%20Relocalization%20+%20Teleoperation/relocalize_confirm.png?width=1200&amp;name=relocalize_confirm.png 1200w"
sizes="(max-width: 400px) 100vw, 400px" width="400"
